Enumerate all accessible files in /mnt/data, compute hashes/sizes, and package into a ZIP import os, hashlib, json, time, zipfile, pathlib, textwrap root = "/mnt/data" def sha256sum(path, block=65536): h = hashlib.sha256() with open(path, "rb") as f: while True: b = f.read(block) if not b: break h.update(b) return h.hexdigest() all_files = [] for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root): for fn in filenames: p = os.path.join(dirpath, fn) try: st = os.stat(p) h = sha256sum(p) rel = os.path.relpath(p, root) all_files.append( "path": rel, "bytes": st.st_size, "sha256": h, "mtime": st.st_mtime ) except Exception as e: all_files.append( "path": os.path.relpath(p, root), "error": str(e) ) # Split the existing canvas export zip into manageable parts with checksums. # If the main zip doesn't exist, recreate it first (lightweight re-pack). import os, json, hashlib, csv, zipfile from datetime import datetime BASE = "/mnt/data" ZIP_MAIN = os.path.join(BASE, "canvas_export_all_assets.zip") PART_PREFIX = os.path.join(BASE, "canvas_export_all_assets.zip.part") MANIFEST_PARTS_JSON = os.path.join(BASE, "canvas_export_parts_manifest.json") MANIFEST_PARTS_CSV = os.path.join(BASE, "canvas_export_parts_manifest.csv") # Choose a conservative part size (in bytes) PART_SIZE = 200 * 1024 * 1024 # 200 MB def sha256(path, chunk_size=1024*1024): h = hashlib.sha256() with open(path, "rb") as f: while True: b = f.read(chunk_size) if not b: break h.update(b) return h.hexdigest() # (Re)create the main zip if missing def ensure_main_zip(): if os.path.exists(ZIP_MAIN): return # Build a simple zip of all files under /mnt/data excluding the output zip and any existing parts with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_MAIN, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z: for root, dirs, files in os.walk(BASE): for name in files: src = os.path.join(root, name) # Skip itself and any previous parts or manifests if os.path.abspath(src) == os.path.abspath(ZIP_MAIN): continue if src.startswith(PART_PREFIX): continue if os.path.basename(src) in ( os.path.basename(MANIFEST_PARTS_JSON), os.path.basename(MANIFEST_PARTS_CSV), ): continue arcname = os.path.relpath(src, BASE) z.write(src, arcname) def split_file(path, part_prefix, part_size): parts = [] total = os.path.getsize(path) idx = 1 with open(path, "rb") as f: while True: chunk = f.read(part_size) if not chunk: break part_path = f" part_prefix idx:02d " with open(part_path, "wb") as pf: pf.write(chunk) parts.append(part_path) idx += 1 return parts, total # Ensure main zip exists ensure_main_zip() # Remove any old parts manifest/parts to avoid confusion for root, dirs, files in os.walk(BASE): for name in files: p = os.path.join(root, name) if p.startswith(PART_PREFIX): try: os.remove(p) except: pass # Split the file parts, total_size = split_file(ZIP_MAIN, PART_PREFIX, PART_SIZE) # Compute checksums and sizes rows = [] for i, p in enumerate(parts, start=1): rows.append( "part_index": i, "filename": os.path.basename(p), "path": p, "size_bytes": os.path.getsize(p), "sha256": sha256(p) ) # Write manifests with open(MANIFEST_PARTS_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as jf: json.dump( "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(), "source_zip": ZIP_MAIN, "source_size_bytes": total_size, "part_size_target_bytes": PART_SIZE, "parts": rows , jf, indent=2) with open(MANIFEST_PARTS_CSV, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as cf: w = csv.writer(cf) w.writerow(["part_index", "filename", "size_bytes", "sha256"]) for r in rows: w.writerow([r["part_index"], r["filename"], r["size_bytes"], r["sha256"]]) # Print a simple summary for the chat UI summary = "source_zip": ZIP_MAIN, "source_size_bytes": total_size, "parts_count": len(parts), "part_size_target_bytes": PART_SIZE, "parts": [ "index": r["part_index"], "filename": r["filename"], "size_bytes": r["size_bytes"], "sha256": r["sha256"] for r in rows], "manifests": "json": MANIFEST_PARTS_JSON, "csv": MANIFEST_PARTS_CSV print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2)) **Assistant (2025-09-29 20:31:32 UTC):** Done.

(full 64-hex here)" , "prov:wasDerivedFrom": "@id": "ipld://bafy..." , "prov:wasAttributedTo": "@id": "https://miamisao.gov/" , "skos:inScheme": "https://example.org/vocab/offenses", "tags": ["writ", "Ch82", "§83.67"] ``` ### 2.2 EmailMessage shard (with quote-level provenance + RDF-star idea) ```json "@context": "schema":"https://schema.org/","prov":"http://www.w3.org/ns/prov#" , "@type": "schema:EmailMessage", "id": "EML-2024-11-28-sao-1", "schema:dateSent": "2024-11-28T14:22:13-05:00", "schema:sender": "@type":"schema:Person","schema:name":"Joshua Durden","schema:email":"mailto:jojidurde@gmail.com" , "schema:recipient": [ "@type":"schema:Organization","schema:name":"State Attorney's Office","schema:email":"mailto:felonyservice@miamisao.com" ], "schema:identifier": "Message-ID:<...>", "hash": "algo":"SHA-256","value":"..." , "body": "Full email body text...", "claims": [ "triple": ["EVT-0609pepper","cites","LAW-83.67"], "valid_time": "at":"2024-11-28" , "record_time": "2024-11-28T14:22:13-05:00", "prov:wasAttributedTo": "@id":"EML-2024-11-28-sao-1" ] ``` > The `claims[]` array is your practical stand-in for RDF-star—“statements about statements” tied back to their *source* and *time*. ### 2.3 Edges (temporal + typed) ```json "source": "FIL-def9876543", "target": "LAW-Soldal", "type": "cites", "valid_time": "from": "2025-04-22", "to": null , "record_time": "2025-04-22T10:01:00-05:00", "prov:wasAttributedTo": "EML-2025-04-22-filing" ``` --- ## 3) Canon integrity & dedup (content-addressing + hashes) - **Compute SHA-256** for every artifact (pdf, audio, mbox, txt, image) and store in `hash.value`. (Use the full 64-hex and display it in the UI.) NIST FIPS 180-4 documents SHA-256; NIST is moving to deprecate SHA-1 entirely. citeturn11search9turn11search7 - **Content-address URIs** (`ipld://`) enable deduplication and immutable references across snapshots.

IPLD provides the Merkle-DAG model behind this. citeturn8search2 --- ## 4) Time that actually works (bi-temporal) Track: - **valid_time** (when a fact is true in the world) - **record_time** (when you learned/recorded it) This bi-temporal pattern resolves “we learned on Friday about a Tuesday event,” and it’s compatible with event sourcing and time-travel storage. (See IBM’s bitemporal primer.) citeturn7search0 --- ## 5) Tagging that scales (SKOS + folksonomy mapping) - Model your controlled terms as SKOS: `Concept` (e.g., “Unlawful Detainer”), `altLabel` (e.g., “Ch82”), `broader/narrower` (e.g., Offense → Housing). citeturn3search0 - Let users tag freely (folksonomy), then **map free tags → SKOS concepts** to keep the graph clean while preserving user language. - Use **schema.org** types for interop (`EmailMessage`, `Legislation`, `Person`, `Event`). citeturn3search1 --- ## 6) Lineage of processing (so courts trust transformations) Declare lineage for extractions (e.g., “CASE.mbox → parse → EML-shards → Evidence Packet”).

FIPS 180-4 governs SHA-256. citeturn11search9 --- ## 11) Why these choices are reliable - **Standards** (PROV-O, SKOS, JSON-LD) are W3C recommendations with wide adoption. citeturn0open0turn3search0turn10search0 - **Temporal graphs** and **GraphRAG** are current research trends for reasoning over evolving facts. citeturn2search0turn5search1 - **Lineage** and **versioned data** are industry-hardened (OpenLineage 1.0; Delta Lake time travel). citeturn6search0turn4search2 - **Content addressing** ensures dedup and integrity from first principles. citeturn8search2 --- ## 12) Immediate next steps (no waiting on backends) 1. **Augment shards** in your canvas with `prov`, `hash`, and `claims` (fields above). 2. **Add time filters** (`valid_time`, `record_time`) to your existing `queryFilter`. 3. **Show provenance chips** and **edge aging** in the UI. 4. **Export a manifest** with per-shard SHA-256 + root manifest hash. 5. **Pilot GraphRAG**: when a user clicks “Explain”, retrieve the dated path + quoted bodies from `claims` only, then render. citeturn5search1 If you want, I can fold these directly into the canvas code in one additive pass (keeping all IDs, links, and prior metadata intact) and produce a tiny sample dataset showing the new fields in action. > **User (2025-10-02 18:26:35 UTC):** > > Can you add all of these to canvas and add to shattercore and update shards accordingly? **Assistant (2025-10-02 18:29:29 UTC):** Done.

It is safe to share. ## Quick Start ```bash # (optional) create virtual env python -m venv .venv && source .venv/bin/activate # install pip install -e . # init a new project root shattercore init --project-name "Mayorgate" --root ./canon # create an atomic shard (content-free sample) shattercore new-shard \ --type "Evidence" \ --title "Placeholder Evidence Root" \ --project "Mayorgate" # list shards shattercore list --project "Mayorgate" # export link tree + schema shattercore export --project "Mayorgate" --out ./export ``` ## Design Overview - **Atomic Shards**: Small, self-contained JSON files stored under `canon/shards////.json`. - **Spelled-Out Types**: `Evidence`, `Timeline`, `People`, `Organization`, `Location`, `LegalCitation`, `Narrative`, `Correspondence`, `Media`, `Policy`, `Process`, `Task`, `Complaint`, `CallLog`, `Incident`, `Finding`, `Request`, `Note`. - **Link Trees**: Stored in `canon/link_trees/*.json` with **meticulous nesting**: - `root` → `children[]` each node references shard IDs, labels, and optional constraints. - **Tagging**: Free-form tags plus controlled vocab (configurable in `config/shattercore.config.yaml`). - **Guards**: Project scope/namespace enforcement; rejects shards that mention disallowed scopes. - **Extensibility**: Add new shard types, validators, or storage backends without changing existing data. ## Project Structure ``` src/shattercore/ cli.py # CLI entrypoints core.py # High-level orchestrator schemas.py # Pydantic models for Shards, LinkTree, Config features/ linktree.py # Link tree creation, validation, traversal tagging.py # Controlled + freeform tags indexing.py # Search + index helpers importers/ mbox.py # (stub) MBOX importer interface storage/ filesystem.py # Filesystem backend (default) tests/ # Unit tests (stubs) config/ shattercore.config.yaml # Default config (no content) examples/ # empty; safe to share data/ # runtime cache/temp (empty) ``` ## Notes - This is a **skeleton**.

Functions are implemented to the point of creating/validating empty shards and exporting schemas. - No user data is bundled or referenced. """ open(f" base /README.md","w").write(readme) # pyproject pyproject = """\ [build-system] requires = ["setuptools>=68", "wheel"] build-backend = "setuptools.build_meta" [project] name = "shattercore" version = "0.0.0" description = "ShatterCore content-free skeleton (atomic shards, link trees, canon guards)" authors = [ name="ShatterCore Maintainers" ] requires-python = ">=3.9" dependencies = [ "pydantic>=2.6.0", "pyyaml>=6.0.1", "click>=8.1.7", ] [project.scripts] shattercore = "shattercore.cli:main" [tool.setuptools.packages.find] where = ["src"] """ open(f" base /pyproject.toml","w").write(pyproject) # schemas.py schemas = """\ from __future__ import annotations from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, ConfigDict from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Literal import hashlib ShardType = Literal[ "Evidence", "Timeline", "People", "Organization", "Location", "LegalCitation", "Narrative", "Correspondence", "Media", "Policy", "Process", "Task", "Complaint", "CallLog", "Incident", "Finding", "Request", "Note", ] class Shard(BaseModel): model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid") project: str = Field(..., description="Project namespace, e.g., 'Mayorgate'") shard_type: ShardType title: str summary: Optional[str] = None tags: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list) links: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="References to other shard IDs") body: Optional[str] = None # intentionally optional; skeleton uses no content id: Optional[str] = None def assign_deterministic_id(self) -> None: basis = f" self.project | self.shard_type | self.title ".strip().lower().encode("utf-8") self.id = hashlib.sha256(basis).hexdigest() class LinkNode(BaseModel): model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid") label: str shard_id: Optional[str] = None children: List["LinkNode"] = Field(default_factory=list) LinkNode.model_rebuild() class LinkTree(BaseModel): model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid") project: str root: LinkNode class ProjectConfig(BaseModel): model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid") project_name: str = "Mayorgate" allowed_scopes: List[str] = ["Mayorgate"] disallowed_keywords: List[str] = [ "Viscera", "Low Life", "LOW LIFE", "VISCERA" # storage/filesystem.py filesystem = """\ from __future__ import annotations import json, os, datetime, re from typing import Optional, List from . import * from ..schemas import Shard, LinkTree, ProjectConfig class FileSystemStorage: def __init__(self, root: str): self.root = os.path.abspath(root) os.makedirs(self.root, exist_ok=True) os.makedirs(self.shard_dir(), exist_ok=True) os.makedirs(self.linktree_dir(), exist_ok=True) def shard_dir(self) -> str: return os.path.join(self.root, "shards") def linktree_dir(self) -> str: return os.path.join(self.root, "link_trees") def write_shard(self, shard: Shard) -> str: # path by type/YYYY/MM/slug.json date = datetime.datetime.utcnow() safe_title = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9-]+","-", shard.title.strip().lower()) path = os.path.join(self.shard_dir(), shard.shard_type, f" date.year ", f" date.month:02d ") os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True) fn = os.path.join(path, f" safe_title .json") with open(fn, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f: json.dump(shard.model_dump(), f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) return fn def list_shards(self) -> List[str]: out = [] for root,_,files in os.walk(self.shard_dir()): for fn in files: if fn.endswith(".json"): out.append(os.path.join(root, fn)) return sorted(out) def write_linktree(self, lt: LinkTree, name: str = "default") -> str: fn = os.path.join(self.linktree_dir(), f" name .json") with open(fn, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f: json.dump(lt.model_dump(), f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) return fn """ open(f" storage /filesystem.py","w").write(filesystem) # """ open(f" importers /mbox.py","w").write(mbox) # core.py core = """\ from __future__ import annotations import os, json, yaml from typing import Optional, List from .schemas import Shard, LinkTree, ProjectConfig from .storage.filesystem import FileSystemStorage from .features.linktree import make_default_linktree class ShatterCore: def __init__(self, project_root: str, config_path: Optional[str] = None): self.project_root = os.path.abspath(project_root) os.makedirs(self.project_root, exist_ok=True) self.config_path = config_path or os.path.join(self.project_root, "config", "shattercore.config.yaml") if os.path.exists(self.config_path): self.cfg = ProjectConfig(**yaml.safe_load(open(self.config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8"))) else: self.cfg = ProjectConfig() os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.config_path), exist_ok=True) yaml.safe_dump(self.cfg.model_dump(), open(self.config_path,"w", encoding="utf-8")) self.storage = FileSystemStorage(os.path.join(self.project_root, "canon")) # Guards against off-scope content def validate_scope(self, shard: Shard) -> None: title_lower = shard.title.lower() for bad in (w.lower() for w in self.cfg.disallowed_keywords): if bad in title_lower: raise ValueError(f"Shard title contains disallowed keyword: bad ") if shard.project not in self.cfg.allowed_scopes: raise ValueError(f"Project scope ' shard.project ' is not allowed by configuration.") def new_shard(self, shard: Shard) -> str: self.validate_scope(shard) shard.assign_deterministic_id() return self.storage.write_shard(shard) def init_linktree(self, name: str = "default") -> str: lt = make_default_linktree(self.cfg.project_name) return self.storage.write_linktree(lt, name=name) def export_schema(self, out_dir: str) -> None: os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True) open(os.path.join(out_dir, "project_config.yaml"),"w", encoding="utf-8").write( yaml.safe_dump(self.cfg.model_dump()) ) schema_info = "Shard": Shard.model_json_schema(), "LinkTree": LinkTree.model_json_schema(), "ProjectConfig": self.cfg.model_dump(), open(os.path.join(out_dir,"schemas.json"),"w", encoding="utf-8").write( json.dumps(schema_info, indent=2) ) """ open(f" src /core.py","w").write(core) # cli.py cli = """\ from __future__ import annotations import click, os, json from .core import ShatterCore from .schemas import Shard @click.group() def main(): \"\"\"ShatterCore CLI (content-free skeleton)\"\"\" pass @main.command("init") @click.option("--project-name", required=True, help="Project namespace (e.g., Mayorgate)") @click.option("--root", default=".", help="Project root directory") def init(project_name: str, root: str): sc = ShatterCore(project_root=root) sc.cfg.project_name = project_name sc.cfg.allowed_scopes = [project_name] sc.export_schema(os.path.join(root, "export")) lt_path = sc.init_linktree() click.echo(f\"Initialized project at os.path.abspath(root) \ tree: lt_path \") @main.command("new-shard") @click.option("--type", "shard_type", required=True) @click.option("--title", required=True) @click.option("--project", required=True) @click.option("--summary", default=None) def new_shard(shard_type: str, title: str, project: str, summary: str|None): sc = ShatterCore(project_root=".") shard = Shard(project=project, shard_type=shard_type, title=title, summary=summary) path = sc.new_shard(shard) click.echo(f\"Wrote shard: path \") @main.command("list") @click.option("--project", default=None, help="(reserved)") def list_cmd(project: str|None): sc = ShatterCore(project_root=".") for p in sc.storage.list_shards(): click.echo(p) @main.command("export") @click.option("--project", default=None, help="(reserved)") @click.option("--out", required=True) def export(project: str|None, out: str): sc = ShatterCore(project_root=".") sc.export_schema(out) click.echo(f\"Exported schemas to os.path.abspath(out) \") """ open(f" src /cli.py","w").write(cli) # """) w("src/shattercore/graph/graph.py", """ import networkx as nx from typing import Iterable, Dict, Any from ..core.models import Shard, Link def build_graph(shards: Iterable[Shard], links: Iterable[Link]) -> nx.DiGraph: g = nx.DiGraph() for s in shards: g.add_node(s.id, title=s.title, namespace=s.namespace, tags=tuple(s.tags)) for l in links: g.add_edge(l.src, l.dst, type=l.type, id=l.id) return g def to_tree(g: nx.DiGraph, root_ids=None) -> Dict[str, Any]: # Simplistic projection: return adjacency for root_ids or all roots = root_ids or [n for n in g.nodes if g.in_degree(n) == 0] def walk(n): return "id": n, "title": g.nodes[n].get("title"), "namespace": g.nodes[n].get("namespace"), "children": [walk(m) for m in g.successors(n)] return "roots": [walk(r) for r in roots] """) w("src/shattercore/plugins/base.py", """ from typing import List, Iterable, Protocol from ..core.models import Shard, Link class BasePlugin(Protocol): name: str def import_items(self, payload: bytes) -> Iterable[Shard]: ... def export_items(self, shards: List[Shard]) -> bytes: ... def enrich(self, shards: List[Shard]) -> List[Shard]: ... """) w("src/shattercore/api/app.py", """ from fastapi import FastAPI from pydantic import BaseModel from typing import List from ..storage.fs import FileStorage from ..core.models import Shard, Link from ..graph.graph import build_graph, to_tree from ..config import default_config as cfg app = FastAPI(title="ShatterCore API", version="0.1.0") store = FileStorage(cfg.storage.root_dir) class LinkCreate(BaseModel): src: str dst: str type: str @app.get("/health") def health(): return "status": "ok" @app.post("/shards", response_model=Shard) def create_shard(shard: Shard): store.add_shard(shard) return shard @app.get("/shards", response_model=List[Shard]) def list_shards(): return list(store.iter_shards()) @app.get("/shards/ sid ", response_model=Shard) def get_shard(sid: str): for s in store.iter_shards(): if s.id == sid: return s return "detail": "not found" # simple fallback @app.post("/links", response_model=Link) def create_link(payload: LinkCreate): link = Link(src=payload.src, dst=payload.dst, type=payload.type) store.add_link(link) return link @app.get("/graph") def graph_summary(): g = build_graph(store.iter_shards(), store.iter_links()) return to_tree(g) """) w("src/shattercore/cli.py", """ import typer, json from pathlib import Path from .core.models import Shard, Link from .storage.fs import FileStorage from .storage.zipper import export_repo_zip from .config import default_config as cfg from typing import Optional app = typer.Typer(help="ShatterCore CLI — manage shards & link trees") @app.command() def init(path: str = typer.Option("./repo", help="Repository root directory")): """Initialize a repository folder with empty JSONL files.""" store = FileStorage(Path(path)) typer.echo(f"Initialized repository at: Path(path).resolve() ") @app.command() def add_shard( title: str = typer.Option(..., help="Title for the shard"), namespace: str = typer.Option(..., help="Namespace (spelled out, e.g.

This repo includes no case content; only program code, docs, and tests. ## Highlights - Python package with **FastAPI** (REST) and **Typer** (CLI) - **Atomic Shards**: normalized units with typed metadata - **Link Trees / Graph**: bidirectional typed edges, namespaced tags - **Namespaces** (spelled out): `EVIDENCE`, `TIMELINE`, `PEOPLE`, `LOCATIONS`, `CORRESPONDENCE`, `LEGAL`, `MEDIA`, `SYSTEM` - **Storage Adapters**: filesystem (JSONL), ZIP bundle export - **Plugin Hooks**: light-weight entrypoints for importers/exporters - **Tests**: basic unit tests and integration smoke tests - Completely content-free; safe to publish as an app template ## Quickstart ```bash pip install -e .[all] uvicorn shattercore.api.app:app --reload shattercore --help ``` See `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` for module map and extension points. API w("src/shattercore/api/app.py", """ from fastapi import FastAPI from pydantic import BaseModel from typing import List from ..storage.fs import FileStorage from ..core.models import Shard, Link from ..graph.graph import build_graph, to_tree from ..config import default_config as cfg app = FastAPI(title='ShatterCore API', version='0.1.0') store = FileStorage(cfg.storage.root_dir) class LinkCreate(BaseModel): src: str dst: str type: str @app.get('/health') def health(): return 'status':'ok' @app.post('/shards', response_model=Shard) def create_shard(shard: Shard): store.add_shard(shard) return shard @app.get('/shards', response_model=List[Shard]) def list_shards(): return list(store.iter_shards()) @app.post('/links', response_model=Link) def create_link(payload: LinkCreate): link = Link(src=payload.src, dst=payload.dst, type=payload.type) store.add_link(link) return link @app.get('/graph') def graph_summary(): g = build_graph(store.iter_shards(), store.iter_links()) return to_tree(g) """) # export const CanonicalTag = z.object( key: z.string(), // e.g., "Domain/Entity", "Theme/Corruption" value: z.string() ); export const AtomicShardBase = z.object( id: z.string(), shardType: z.enum([ "Evidence", "Timeline", "Person", "Organization", "Location", "Event", "LegalReference", "Note" ]), title: z.string(), summary: z.string().optional(), createdAtIso: z.string().optional(), updatedAtIso: z.string().optional(), originatesFrom: z.string().optional(), // source system, file, or ingestion module tags: z.array(CanonicalTag).default([]), links: z.array(LinkRef).default([]) ); export const EvidenceShard = AtomicShardBase.extend( shardType: z.literal("Evidence"), evidenceMetadata: z.object( sourceType: z.enum(["Email", "Transcript", "Audio", "Image", "ManualEntry", "Other"]).optional(), sourceUriOrPath: z.string().optional(), checksumSha256: z.string().optional(), capturedAtIso: z.string().optional(), ), textBody: z.string().optional() ); export const TimelineShard = AtomicShardBase.extend( shardType: z.literal("Timeline"), timelineMetadata: z.object( occurredAtIso: z.string(), locationApprox: z.string().optional() ), narrative: z.string().optional() ); export type Shard = z.infer; """ linktree_ts = """\ /** * LinkTree model: typed backlinks, tag hierarchies, facets */ import Graph from "graphology"; import Shard from "./schema.shard"; export type LinkEdge = from: string; to: string; linkType: string; ; export class LinkTree graph: Graph; constructor() this.graph = new Graph( multi: true, type: "directed" ); addShard(shard: Shard) if (!this.graph.hasNode(shard.id)) this.graph.addNode(shard.id, shard ); for (const l of shard.links ?? []) if (!this.graph.hasNode(l.id)) this.graph.addNode(l.id); this.graph.addEdgeWithKey(`$ shard.id ->$ l.id :$ l.linkType `, shard.id, l.id, linkType: l.linkType ); backlinks(id: string): string[] const res: string[] = []; this.graph.forEachInEdge(id, (edge, attrs, source) => res.push(source as string); ); return res; outgoing(id: string): string[] const res: string[] = []; this.graph.forEachOutEdge(id, (edge, attrs, _source, target) => res.push(target as string); ); return res; """ pipeline_ts = """\ /** * Shard Processing Pipeline (ingest → normalize → link → index → validate) * Content-free, with stubs to extend. */ import z from "zod"; import Shard from "../models/schema.shard"; export type PipelineStageName = | "ingest" | "normalize" | "link" | "index" | "validate"; export type PipelineContext = shards: Shard[]; warnings: string[]; errors: string[]; ; export type Stage = name: PipelineStageName; run(ctx: PipelineContext): Promise; ; export class Pipeline stages: Stage[] = []; use(stage: Stage) this.stages.push(stage); return this; async run(initial: PipelineContext): Promise let ctx = initial; for (const s of this.stages) ctx = await s.run(ctx); return ctx; // --- Default stage stubs --- export const IngestStage: Stage = name: "ingest", async run(ctx) // TODO: load shards from importers (mbox, transcript) – skeleton only return ctx; ; """ index_ts = """\ import Pipeline, IngestStage, NormalizeStage, LinkStage, IndexStage, ValidateStage from "./pipeline/pipeline"; import readFileSync from "node:fs"; async function main() const pipeline = new Pipeline() .use(IngestStage) .use(NormalizeStage) .use(LinkStage) .use(IndexStage) .use(ValidateStage); const result = await pipeline.run( shards: [], warnings: [], errors: [] ); console.log("Pipeline completed.", shardCount: result.shards.length, warnings: result.warnings.length, errors: result.errors.length ); main().catch(err => console.error(err); process.exit(1); ); """ importer_mbox_ts = """\ /** * Placeholder: MBOX importer (skeleton) * Implement RFC822 parsing and produce Evidence shards. */ export async function importFromMbox(_mboxPath: string) return []; ``` - **Python** ```bash pip install google-genai ``` ```py from google import genai client = genai.Client(api_key=os.environ["GEMINI_API_KEY"]) print(client.models.generate_content(model="gemini-2.5-flash", contents="ping").text) ``` (Official library + quickstart snippets are in Google’s docs.) citeturn7search0 **4) (Optional) Upload large prompt/config files via the Files API** If you want ShatterCore to reference spec/prompt files (JSON/YAML, PDFs, etc.) without inlining them, upload once and reuse the returned `file.uri` in requests. - **JS example**: ```ts import GoogleGenAI, createUserContent, createPartFromUri from "@google/genai"; ``` (Files API supports listing, getting, deleting; 20 GB per project; 2 GB/file; files auto-expire—see docs.) citeturn9search1turn9search0 **5) Prototype prompts in AI Studio, then click “Get code”** - In AI Studio, create a prompt (chat or structured). - Use **Get code** to copy a ready-to-run snippet (JS/Python).

be careful):** ```bash rm -rf myfolder ``` ### Patterns & hidden files - **Delete all `.log` files in the current dir:** ```bash rm *.log ``` - **Delete all files starting with `temp_`:** ```bash rm temp_* ``` - **Delete hidden files (start with a dot):** ```bash rm .*.bak ``` (Or show them first: `ls -la`) ### Confirm before mass delete - **Preview with `find`:** ```bash find . -maxdepth 1 -type f -name "*.tmp" ``` - **Delete the results safely (interactive):** ```bash find . -type f -name "*.tmp" -exec rm -i + ``` ### Common Android storage paths If you’ve granted Termux **storage permission** (`termux-setup-storage`), you’ll see: - `~/storage/downloads` → your phone’s **Downloads** - `~/storage/shared` → **Internal storage** root (same as `/sdcard`) **Examples:** ```bash termux-setup-storage # run once to grant access cd ~/storage/downloads rm -i oldfile.zip rm -rf "ShatterCore Old Build" ``` ### Permissions & “Operation not permitted” - If you get `EACCES` / permission errors in shared storage: - Make sure you ran `termux-setup-storage` and accepted the Android dialog. - Stay **inside** `~/storage/...` when removing files on shared storage. - Some system-managed dirs can’t be modified. ### Safety tips - Use `rm -i` until you’re 100% confident. - Double-check your path with `pwd` and contents with `ls -la` before `rm -rf`. - Quote names with spaces: ```bash rm "file with spaces.txt" ``` ### Optional: “Trash” instead of permanent delete If you want a recycle-bin-style workflow: ```bash pkg install trash-cli trash-put myfile.txt # send to trash trash-list # see trashed files trash-restore # restore interactively trash-empty # empty trash ``` If you tell me the exact path of what you want to remove, I’ll give you the precise command. > **User (2025-10-03 22:17:34 UTC):** > > Give me updated full code for root package.json file **Assistant (2025-10-03 22:17:44 UTC):** Got you.

So the flow is: - Create an API key in AI Studio - Paste the snippet (or your own @google/genai code) into your ShatterCore project - Run locally (Termux) or deploy (optional) Here’s the exact, step-by-step on Android/Termux: # 1) Create your AI Studio API key 1.

It wires up environment, basic CLI flags, initializes the ShatterCore pipeline, and runs it. ```ts /** * ShatterCore — App Entrypoint * ----------------------------------------- * Compatible with: ts-node / ts-node-dev * Module target: commonjs * * Responsibilities: * - Load environment (optional .env) * - Parse minimal CLI flags * - Resolve input/output paths * - Initialize and run the ShatterCore pipeline * - Graceful shutdown & structured logging */ import fs from "node:fs"; function out(minLevel: LogLevel, tag: string, msg: string, meta?: unknown) if (rank[minLevel] < current) return; const line = `[$ stamp() ] [$ tag ] $ msg `; if (meta !== undefined) // eslint-disable-next-line no-console console.log(line, meta); else // eslint-disable-next-line no-console console.log(line); return level, debug: (msg, meta) => out("debug", "DEBUG", msg, meta), info: (msg, meta) => out("info", "INFO ", msg, meta), warn: (msg, meta) => out("warn", "WARN ", msg, meta), error: (msg, meta) => out("error", "ERROR", msg, meta), ; else const k = token.slice(2); if (args[0] && !args[0].startsWith("--")) out[k] = args.shift()!; else out[k] = true; return out; /** ---------- Resolve Paths Safely ---------- */ function resolveDir(p: string) const full = path.resolve(process.cwd(), p); catch (err) opts.logger?.warn( "Could not load real pipeline; using fallback skeleton pipeline.", error: (err as Error)?.message ); return createFallbackPipeline(opts); /** ---------- Fallback Pipeline (No-Op but Useful) ---------- */ function createFallbackPipeline(opts: ShatterCorePipelineOptions): ShatterCorePipeline const logger, inputDir, outputDir, dryRun = opts; "true" : "false" `, "", "Create your real pipeline in src/pipeline/pipeline.ts exporting either:", "- export function createShatterCorePipeline(opts) => ShatterCorePipeline", "- or export class ShatterCorePipeline implements initialize, run, shutdown ", "", ].join(" "), "utf8" ); , async run() logger?.info("FallbackPipeline.run() — no-op execution."); const note = path.join(outputDir, "_fallback_run.txt"); fs.writeFileSync( note, `run: $ new Date().toISOString() (no-op) `, encoding: "utf8", flag: "a" ); // Pretend we processed something: if (!dryRun) const out = path.join(outputDir, "artifacts"); fs.mkdirSync(out, recursive: true ); fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, "example.json"), JSON.stringify( ok: true , null, 2)); , async shutdown() logger?.info("FallbackPipeline.shutdown() — graceful cleanup complete."); , ; /** ---------- Main ---------- */ async function main() // Read env with sane fallbacks const LOG_LEVEL = (process.env.LOG_LEVEL as LogLevel) ?? "info"; const DEFAULT_INPUT = process.env.SC_INPUT_DIR ?? "data/input"; const DEFAULT_OUTPUT = process.env.SC_OUTPUT_DIR ?? "data/output"; const DEFAULT_TEMP = process.env.SC_TEMP_DIR ?? LOG_LEVEL) as LogLevel); const logger = createLogger(logLevel); logger.info("ShatterCore starting…", inputDir, outputDir, tempDir, dryRun, logLevel, node: process.version, pid: process.pid, ); // Guard rails: ensure input/output exist for (const d of [inputDir, outputDir, tempDir]) if (!fs.existsSync(d)) fs.mkdirSync(d, recursive: true ); // Load real pipeline if present, otherwise fallback const pipeline = await loadPipeline( inputDir, outputDir, tempDir, dryRun, logger, ); // Graceful shutdown hooks const shutdown = async (signal: string) => try logger.warn(`Received $ signal .

String(err); logger.error("Fatal error", error: msg, stack: (err as Error)?.stack ); process.exitCode = 1; // Only run if this file is the entrypoint (ts-node-dev / ts-node) if (require.main === module) // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-floating-promises main(); Here’s how to wire up a real pipeline so the fallback message goes away and ShatterCore actually does work. --- ## 1) Create `src/pipeline/pipeline.ts` with the expected API The loader in `src/index.ts` is looking for one of these exports: - a **default** class with `initialize(opts)` and `run()` methods, **or** - a named export `createPipeline(opts)` that returns an object with `initialize()` and `run()`.

Easiest is a default class: ```ts // src/pipeline/pipeline.ts import promises as fs from "fs"; import * as path from "path"; export type PipelineInitOptions = inputDir: string; outputDir: string; tempDir: string; dryRun: boolean; log: (level: "info" | "warn" | "error", msg: string, meta?: any) => void; ; export default class ShatterCorePipeline private inputDir!: string; private outputDir!: string; private tempDir!: string; private dryRun!: boolean; private log!: PipelineInitOptions["log"]; async initialize(opts: PipelineInitOptions) this.inputDir = opts.inputDir; this.outputDir = opts.outputDir; this.tempDir = opts.tempDir; this.dryRun = opts.dryRun; this.log = opts.log; // Ensure folders exist await fs.mkdir(this.outputDir, recursive: true ); await fs.mkdir(this.tempDir, recursive: true ); this.log("info", "Pipeline initialized", inputDir: this.inputDir, outputDir: this.outputDir, tempDir: this.tempDir, dryRun: this.dryRun, ); /** * Minimal “real” run: * - Reads every file in data/input * - Writes a stub processed copy to data/output * - You can replace this with your shard/linking logic */ async run() const entries = await fs.readdir(this.inputDir).catch(() => []); if (entries.length === 0) this.log("warn", "No input files found; nothing to process.", inputDir: this.inputDir, ); return; for (const name of entries) const inPath = path.join(this.inputDir, name); const outPath = path.join(this.outputDir, `$ name .processed.json`); try const buf = await fs.readFile(inPath); const text = buf.toString("utf8"); // --- Replace this block with your shard → link → index flow --- const processed = file: name, size: buf.length, processedAt: new Date().toISOString(), notes: "Replace with ShatterCore shard/graph processing.", preview: text.slice(0, 240), ; // -------------------------------------------------------------- if (this.dryRun) this.log("info", "DRY-RUN: would write processed output", outPath, processedKeys: Object.keys(processed), ); else await fs.writeFile(outPath, JSON.stringify(processed, null, 2), "utf8"); this.log("info", "Wrote processed output", outPath ); catch (err: any) this.log("error", "Failed processing input file", name, error: err?.message ); this.log("info", "Pipeline completed."); ``` > If you prefer the factory pattern, export `export function createPipeline(opts) ... ` that returns ` initialize, run `. --- ## 2) Make sure the file path matches the import Your tree shows: ``` src/ index.ts pipeline/ pipeline.ts <-- this is correct ``` And `index.ts` is importing: ```ts const mod = await import("./pipeline/pipeline"); main().catch((err) => // eslint-disable-next-line no-console console.error(err); process.exitCode = 1; ); ``` --- ## What to do now 1) **Save** the three files above (overwrite existing). 2) From your project root in Termux: ```bash rm -rf node_modules package-lock.json npm install npm run dev ``` You should **no longer** see: - `exports is not defined in ES module scope` - `Must use import to load ES Module` If you still get a fallback message, that’s fine until you create `src/pipeline/pipeline.ts` that exports either: ```ts export class ShatterCorePipeline implements IShatterCorePipeline async initialize(options: ShatterCoreOptions) async run() ``` **or** ```ts export function createShatterCorePipeline(_: ShatterCoreOptions): IShatterCorePipeline return async initialize() , async run() ; Paste the file as `src/index.ts`: ```ts import path from "path"; import fs from "fs"; import fileURLToPath from "url"; type LogLevel = "debug" | "info" | "warn" | "error"; function log(level: LogLevel, ...args: any[]) const tag = `[$ level.toUpperCase() ]`; console.log(tag, ...args); /** * Pipeline interface / stub type */ export interface ShatterCorePipeline initialize(opts?: any): Promise | void; run?(opts?: any): Promise | void; /** * Try to load a pipeline module from src/pipeline * Accepts either: * - export default class implements ShatterCorePipeline * - export function createShatterCorePipeline(): ShatterCorePipeline */ async function loadPipeline(): Promise const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url); // check default if (mod?.default) const obj = mod.default; if (typeof obj === "function") // class or factory const inst = new obj(); log("info", "Loaded pipeline via default export (class)."); return inst as ShatterCorePipeline; if (typeof obj === "object") log("info", "Loaded pipeline via default export (object)."); return obj as ShatterCorePipeline; if (typeof mod?.createShatterCorePipeline === "function") const inst = await mod.createShatterCorePipeline(); using fallback skeleton pipeline.", error: (err as Error).message ); // fallback skeleton pipeline: const fallback: ShatterCorePipeline = initialize: async (opts?: any) => log("info", "FallbackPipeline.initialize()", opts ?? ); , run: async () => log("info", "FallbackPipeline.run() — no-op execution."); ; return fallback; async function main() const env = inputDir: path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data", "input"), outputDir: path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data", "output"), tempDir: path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".tmp"), dryRun: false, logLevel: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? Paste verbatim. ```ts // src/index.ts import path from "path"; import fs from "fs"; import fileURLToPath from "url"; const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url); const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename); type PipelineLike = initialize?: (opts?: any) => Promise | void; run?: (opts?: any) => Promise | void; ; async function loadPipeline(): Promise // candidate files in order const candidates = [ path.resolve(__dirname, "pipeline", "index.ts"), path.resolve(__dirname, "pipeline", "index.mjs"), path.resolve(__dirname, "pipeline", "pipeline.ts"), path.resolve(__dirname, "pipeline", "pipeline.mjs"), path.resolve(__dirname, "pipeline", "fallback-pipeline.ts") ]; for (const c of candidates) if (fs.existsSync(c)) try // dynamic import works with file:// for ESM; convert to URL const m = await import(pathToFileURL(c).href); // prefer exported default, then named exports const exported = m?.default ?? m?.ShatterCorePipeline ?? m?.createShatterCorePipeline ?? m; // If it's a class/factory, instantiate or call if (typeof exported === "function") const inst = exported.length === 0 ? (new exported()) : await exported(); return inst as PipelineLike; return exported as PipelineLike; catch (err) console.warn(`Failed to import pipeline candidate $ c :`, err); // fallback: load bundled fallback pipeline const fallback = await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(__dirname, "pipeline", "fallback-pipeline.ts")).href); return fallback.default ?? fallback.FallbackPipeline ?? fallback; // helper to convert path to file URL function pathToFileURL(p: string) // Node's `url.pathToFileURL` available, but to avoid extra import, create manually: const url = new URL("file://" + p); return url; async function main() console.info("ShatterCore starting…", node: process.version, cwd: process.cwd(), pid: process.pid ); const pipeline = await loadPipeline(); if (!pipeline) console.error("No pipeline available."); process.exit(1); if (pipeline.initialize) await pipeline.initialize( inputDir: path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data", "input"), outputDir: path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data", "output"), dryRun: false, ); if (pipeline.run) await pipeline.run(); else console.info("Pipeline has no run() method — nothing to do."); console.info("ShatterCore finished."); main().catch((err) => console.error("Fatal error:", err); process.exit(1); ); ``` --- ### 6) `src/pipeline/pipeline.ts` (real pipeline skeleton) ```ts // src/pipeline/pipeline.ts export class ShatterCorePipeline private opts: any; constructor(opts: any = ) this.opts = opts; async initialize(opts: any = ) this.opts = ...this.opts, ...opts ; console.info("ShatterCorePipeline.initialize()", this.opts); // initialize directories if needed async run() console.info("ShatterCorePipeline.run() — sample no-op pipeline"); // load files from inputDir, parse, create shards, write to outputDir // This is where you'd implement the shard/core logic. await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200)); console.info("Sample pipeline complete."); export default ShatterCorePipeline; ``` --- ### 7) `src/pipeline/index.ts` (convenience re-export) ```ts // src/pipeline/index.ts export default as ShatterCorePipeline from "./pipeline.js"; export default from "./pipeline.js"; ``` > **Important**: If using TS files, Node resolves `.ts` via `ts-node-dev`.

Create `src/index.ts` exactly as below: ```ts // src/index.ts import path from "path"; import fs from "fs"; import pino from "pino"; const logger = pino( level: process.env.SHATTERCORE_LOG || "info" ); using fallback skeleton pipeline."); return new (class FallbackPipeline async initialize(opts = ) logger.info("FallbackPipeline.initialize()", opts); async run(opts = ) logger.info("FallbackPipeline.run() — no-op execution."); )(); async function main() logger.info("ShatterCore starting…", inputDir: process.env.SC_INPUT_DIR || path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data", "input"), outputDir: process.env.SC_OUTPUT_DIR || path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data", "output"), dryRun: !!process.env.SC_DRY_RUN ); const pipeline = await loadPipeline(); try await pipeline.initialize( inputDir: process.env.SC_INPUT_DIR, outputDir: process.env.SC_OUTPUT_DIR, dryRun: !!process.env.SC_DRY_RUN ); await pipeline.run( ); logger.info("ShatterCore finished."); catch (err) logger.error( err: (err as Error).stack || err , "Pipeline failed"); process.exitCode = 1; if (import.meta.url === `file://$ process.cwd() /src/index.ts` || import.meta.url.endsWith("/src/index.ts")) // run main when executed directly via ts-node-dev main(); I kept it organized so you can scan for the parts you want and act on them. --- # Executive summary (one-line) We designed ShatterCore as an atomic-shard based evidence/timeline system with a pluggable pipeline (importers → sharders → link/graph store → exporters + UI/canvas), added strict naming/abbreviation conventions (SC, EVD, TL, etc.), created a fallback pipeline skeleton and developer tooling (Node/TS + dev script), and produced deployment instructions for GitHub & Google AI Studio and Termux/Android.

Several upload files you sent have expired on the platform — reupload if you want me to extract and re-bake a zip. --- # High-level architecture & goals - **Primary goal:** Preserve every fact/story/event as canonical “canon”, convert everything into atomic **shards**, and make those shards searchable, linkable, and exportable. - **Data model:** *Atomic Shard* = smallest indivisible unit of canon (1 event, 1 email body, 1 transcript segment, 1 metadata block).

Shards are indexed and connected via explicit link trees. - **Core subsystems:** - **SC (ShatterCore)** — main application orchestrator and CLI. - **Pipeline subsystem** — pluggable modules to ingest different sources (mbox, mp3+transcript, drive links, manual JSON, forms). - **Sharder** — converts incoming artifacts into atomic shards, assigns canonical IDs and hashes. - **Linking / Graph store** — link trees that nest shards, tag relationships (EVD, TL, PERSON, LOC, DOC). - **Exporters / Importers** — ZIP, JSONL, HTML, or prepared evidence bundles for courts or defense. - **Canvas/Canon** — human readable repository view (the canvas), where canonical data is stored for review & editing. - **Fallback pipeline** — skeleton pipeline used if real one is missing so the app still runs. --- # Conventions & abbreviations (requested, now canonical) - `SC` = ShatterCore (the app) - `EVD` = Evidence (a top-level tag for evidentiary shards) - `TL` = Timeline (ordered sequence of shards/events) - `SH` = Shard (atomic shard) - `SC_ROOT`, `SC_DATA`, `SC_TMP` — recommended folder names - `CANON` = Canonical dataset (the canonical canvas) - `SHATTERCORE_PIPELINE` = module that implements pipeline API --- # New features / components we discussed and added (detailed) ## 1) **Atomic sharding engine** - Converts documents/emails/transcripts/audio transcripts/complaints into small canonical shards. - Each shard includes: - `id` (UUID) - `type` (email, transcript, complaint, timeline-event, audio-metadata, photo-metadata) - `source` (filename, drive link) - `sha256` hash (content hash) - `created_at` and `captured_at` timestamps - `body_text` (raw) - `metadata` (headers for email; speaker/time for transcript) - `tags` (EVD, TL, PERSON, LOCATION, LEGAL_REF) - `links` (link to parent or related shards) ## 2) **Link trees and nested linkage** - Every shard can have `parents` and `children` and `related` links. - You can produce nested link trees such as: - `TL` (timeline) → day → event → shards (emails/transcripts/bodycam) - Link trees persisted as adjacency lists and also stored as graph edges (for visualizations). ## 3) **ShatterCore (SC) canonical canvas** - A stored “canonical” representation of the whole case (the canvas). - Supports: - Adding / removing canon (explicit keep / prune rules; “retain unless explicitly removed” directive). - Exporting the canvas as a ZIP of shards (shard JSON files) and a master index. ## 4) **Evidence pack formats** - `EVIDENCE_BUNDLE.zip` structure suggestion: - `/manifest.json` (full index and hash list) - `/shards/.json` - `/files/` - `/timeline/` - Hash-verifiable: `manifest.json` contains SHA256 for each file. ## 5) **Fallback pipeline & skeleton pipeline API** - Pipeline loader now tries to import: - `src/pipeline/pipeline` (preferred) - `src/pipeline/index.mjs` fallback - Expected exports: - `export class ShatterCorePipeline` OR - `export function createShatterCorePipe(config): ShatterCorePipeline` - Fallback pipeline is included to avoid runtime failure if custom pipeline missing — it returns a no-op skeleton pipeline. ## 6) **Importer adapters** - Email (mbox) importer: parse full headers + body text, create one shard per message (with headers metadata). - Audio/transcript importer: create both audio metadata shard and transcript text shards; link to each other. - Drive-link importer: store link shard with access info, add manifest entry. - Manual JSON importer: ingest preformatted shards. ## 7) **Shard processing / “shattercore core” flows** - **Extraction** (parse source) → **Normalization** (strip PII per rules if requested) → **Sharding** (create atomic shards) → **Linking** (apply tags & build link trees) → **Export/Store** (persist JSON shards + indexes). ## 8) **Indexing & search** - Text index (simple full-text search on body_text) + tag-based facets (EVD, TL, person) and filters. - Can export the index for offline search. ## 9) **Evidence timeline builder** - Auto-assemble timelines based on timestamp metadata and user overrides. - Support for manual insertion/edits. ## 10) **Export strategies** - Export to: - Zip with full headers and raw bodies (for defense counsel). - Drive link set (manifest that points to Google Drive). - Court-ready print (PDF timeline + exhibits). - Support chunking large exports into multiple zips (you asked for splitting into N parts). ## 11) **ShatterCore project skeleton & dev tooling** - TypeScript + `ts-node-dev` dev script. - `package.json` recommendations to include: -
